<script src="https://assets.adobedtm.com/5d4962a43b79/814eb6e9b4e1/launch-4bc07f1e0b0b.min.js"></script>

<script>
  (function() {
    // Define your SSO/login URL.
    var ssoLoginUrl = "https://docs.api.nvidia.com/login";

    // Helper: Determine if the current page path is versioned (e.g., /v0.1/).
    function isVersionedPath() {
      return /\/v\d+(\.\d+)?\//.test(window.location.pathname);
    }

    // Helper: Create and attach a loading overlay over the error container.
    function addLoadingOverlay(container) {
      if (container) {
        // Ensure the container has relative positioning.
        if (getComputedStyle(container).position === "static") {
          container.style.position = "relative";
        }

        // Create the overlay element.
        var overlay = document.createElement("div");
        overlay.id = "loading-overlay";
        overlay.style.position = "absolute";
        overlay.style.top = 0;
        overlay.style.left = 0;
        overlay.style.right = 0;
        overlay.style.bottom = 0;
        overlay.style.backgroundColor = "rgba(255,255,255,0.8)"; // semi-transparent white
        overlay.style.display = "flex";
        overlay.style.alignItems = "center";
        overlay.style.justifyContent = "center";
        overlay.style.fontSize = "1.5em";
        overlay.style.color = "#333";
        overlay.innerText = "Loading…";
        
        container.appendChild(overlay);
      }
    }

    // Helper: Remove the loading overlay.
    function removeLoadingOverlay() {
      var overlay = document.getElementById("loading-overlay");
      if (overlay && overlay.parentNode) {
        overlay.parentNode.removeChild(overlay);
      }
    }

    // Core function: Check meta tag and login state, and redirect if needed.
    function checkAndRedirect() {
      // Only run on versioned pages.
      if (!isVersionedPath()) {
        return false;
      }
      
      // Verify the meta tag exists indicating a private page.
      var metaDescription = document.querySelector('meta[name="twitter:description"]');
      if (!metaDescription || metaDescription.content.indexOf("This page is password-protected.") === -1) {
        return false;
      }

      // Retrieve and parse the JSON data from the "hub-me" element.
      var hubMeEl = document.getElementById("hub-me");
      if (hubMeEl) {
        try {
          var jsonData = JSON.parse(hubMeEl.getAttribute("data-json"));
          // Redirect if the user is not logged in.
          if (jsonData && jsonData.loggedIn === false) {
            window.location.href = ssoLoginUrl;
            return true; // Redirect triggered.
          }
        } catch (e) {
          console.error("Error parsing login JSON:", e);
        }
      }
      return false;
    }

    // --- Fast Detection via Fetch Interception (for 404 responses) ---
    if (window.fetch) {
      var originalFetch = window.fetch;
      window.fetch = function(input, init) {
        return originalFetch(input, init).then(function(response) {
          // When a 404 response is detected.
          if (!response.ok && response.status === 404) {
            checkAndRedirect();
          }
          return response;
        });
      };
    }

    // --- Fallback: MutationObserver for SPA dynamic updates ---
    function setupObserver() {
      var observer = new MutationObserver(function() {
        checkAndRedirect();
      });
      observer.observe(document.body, {
        childList: true,
        subtree: true
      });
    }

    // On DOMContentLoaded, run the logic.
    document.addEventListener("DOMContentLoaded", function() {
      // Only act on versioned pages.
      if (!isVersionedPath()) {
        return;
      }

      // Select the error container (based on a class starting with "PageNotFound").
      var errorContainer = document.querySelector("div[class^='PageNotFound']");
      if (errorContainer) {
        // Instead of display: none, set visibility to hidden.
        errorContainer.style.visibility = "hidden";
        // Add the loading overlay.
        addLoadingOverlay(errorContainer);
      }
      
      // Run the redirect check. If a redirect occurs, navigation will cancel further steps.
      var redirected = checkAndRedirect();
      
      // Setup the MutationObserver for dynamic SPA updates.
      setupObserver();

      // After a short delay, if no redirect occurred, remove the overlay and show the error container.
      setTimeout(function(){
        if (!redirected && errorContainer) {
          removeLoadingOverlay();
          errorContainer.style.visibility = "visible";
        }
      }, 500);
    });
  })();
</script>

aria-label="Hide sidebar navigation" class="Nav-toggle-collapse39KxgTH727KL" type="button"></button></nav><article class="SuperHubDoc-article3ArTrEavUTKg rm-Article rm-Guides-SuperHub"><header class="rm-PageHeader" id="content-head"><div class="row clearfix"><div class="col-xs-9"><h1 class="rm-PageHeader-title">API Catalog Quickstart Guide</h1></div></div></header><div class="grid-container-fluid" id="content-container"><section class="content-body grid-75 "><div style="display:contents"><div class="rm-Markdown markdown-body rm-Markdown markdown-body ng-non-bindable" data-testid="RDMD" style="margin-left:31px"><style>/*! tailwindcss v4.1.17 | MIT License | https://tailwindcss.com */
@layer theme, base, components, utilities;
@layer utilities;
</style><h2 class="heading heading-2 header-scroll"><div class="heading-anchor anchor waypoint" id="interacting-with-nim-through-the-api-endpoints"></div><div class="heading-text">Interacting with NIM through the API Endpoints</div><a aria-label="Skip link to Interacting with NIM through the API Endpoints" class="heading-anchor-icon fa fa-regular fa-anchor" href="#interacting-with-nim-through-the-api-endpoints"></a></h2>
<p>This Quickstart Guide shows you how to interact with a model that has been hosted as a NIM through a live <a href="https://docs.api.nvidia.com/nim/docs/models" target="" title="">API endpoint</a>, both directly through a web-based UI and through Python code.</p>
<p>The NIM is hosted by NVIDIA on NVIDIA&#x27;s own <a href="https://www.nvidia.com/en-us/data-center/dgx-cloud/" target="" title="">DGX Cloud</a> environment.</p>
<blockquote class="callout callout_default" theme="📒"><span class="callout-icon">📒</span><h3 class="heading heading-3 header-scroll"><div class="heading-anchor anchor waypoint" id="note-this-guide-uses-the-llama-31-8b-instruct-model-but-you-can-follow-the-same-guide-and-use-any-model-available-in-the-nvidia-api-catalog"></div><div class="heading-text">NOTE: This guide uses the llama-3.1-8b-instruct model, but you can follow the same guide and use any model available in the <a href="https://build.nvidia.com/explore/discover" target="" title="">NVIDIA API Catalog</a></div><a aria-label="Skip link to NOTE: This guide uses the llama-3.1-8b-instruct model, but you can follow the same guide and use any model available in the ,[object Object]" class="heading-anchor-icon fa fa-regular fa-anchor" href="#note-this-guide-uses-the-llama-31-8b-instruct-model-but-you-can-follow-the-same-guide-and-use-any-model-available-in-the-nvidia-api-catalog"></a></h3></blockquote>
<h3 class="heading heading-3 header-scroll"><div class="heading-anchor anchor waypoint" id="interacting-through-the-web-based-ui"></div><div class="heading-text">Interacting through the web based UI</div><a aria-label="Skip link to Interacting through the web based UI" class="heading-anchor-icon fa fa-regular fa-anchor" href="#interacting-through-the-web-based-ui"></a></h3>
<p>The following instructions guide you through how to interact with the Llama-3.1-8B-instruct model API using a web-based UI.</p>
<ol>
<li>
<p>Visit <a href="https://build.nvidia.com/explore/discover" target="" title="">build.NVIDIA.com</a> on your web browser.<br/>
This webpage shows you the full catalog of NIM microservices. You can explore by Model type or Industry, or use the search bar to find a specific NIM.</p>
</li>
<li>
<p>Within the search bar, type <em>llama-3</em> and select the <em>meta/llama-3.1-8B-instruct</em> model.</p>
</li>
</ol>
<span aria-label="Expand image" class="img lightbox closed" role="button" tabindex="0"><span class="lightbox-inner"><img alt="" class="img img-align-center " height="auto" loading="lazy" src="https://files.readme.io/eb5ebd9d5807c24121019632907e36211f88291b8caca48e26e1bbc3b8c2de9d-Screenshot_2025-03-18_at_18.18.17.png" title="" width="auto"/></span></span>
<p>This will take you to the model page. You may see a warning regarding the use of 3rd Party models. Click <em>Acknowledge &amp; Continue</em> to proceed.</p>
<ol start="3">
<li>In the <em>Preview</em> tab on the left of the page you can interact with the NIM through the web UI. Try clicking on one of the example questions, or write your own question where you are prompted to <em>Type text here</em> then click <em>Send</em>.</li>
</ol>
<span aria-label="Expand image" class="img lightbox closed" role="button" tabindex="0"><span class="lightbox-inner"><img alt="" class="img img-align-center " height="auto" loading="lazy" src="https://files.readme.io/a0257ae2938023cc713037e1d236e10fd8fc3029ce626e857b25ad6fbf67115f-Screenshot_2025-03-18_at_19.59.01.png" title="" width="auto"/></span></span>
<p>Your request will be sent to the model API which is hosted on NVIDIA DGX Cloud, and the model response will appear in the <em>Preview</em>    box below your request.</p>
<h3 class="heading heading-3 header-scroll"><div class="heading-anchor anchor waypoint" id="interacting-through-python"></div><div class="heading-text">Interacting through Python</div><a aria-label="Skip link to Interacting through Python" class="heading-anchor-icon fa fa-regular fa-anchor" href="#interacting-through-python"></a></h3>
<p>You can also interact with the <em>llama-3.1-8b-instruct</em> model API endpoint using Python. Follow the instructions below to set up an API Key and send a request to a model.</p>
<p>If you already have an NVIDIA Developer API Key, skip to step 4.</p>
<ol>
<li>In the right hand pane of the <a href="https://build.nvidia.com/meta/llama-3_1-8b-instruct" target="" title="">model page</a>,  click <em>Get API Key</em>.</li>
</ol>
<span aria-label="Expand image" class="img lightbox closed" role="button" tabindex="0"><span class="lightbox-inner"><img alt="" class="img img-align-center " height="auto" loading="lazy" src="https://files.readme.io/414f8e8a8f789a527c36766da41241066c9c49e68db7cbc359f7210f965c6ea5-Screenshot_2025-03-18_at_18.10.58.png" title="" width="auto"/></span></span>
<ol start="2">
<li>Enter your email ID in the pop-up window, and click Next. By signing up, you will become a member of the Developer Program.</li>
</ol>
<span aria-label="Expand image" class="img lightbox closed" role="button" tabindex="0"><span class="lightbox-inner"><img alt="" class="img img-align-center " height="auto" loading="lazy" src="https://files.readme.io/57f6b1f17991fc520adfd1c05edf792b7171d23fa2c15145d75857701af474a6-Screenshot_2025-03-18_at_18.11.17.png" title="" width="auto"/></span></span>
<p>If you already have an NVIDIA Account, enter the email address associated with the account at this step. You will then be prompted to sign in.</p>
<p>If you do not already have an NVIDIA Account you will be prompted to set a Password. Follow the on-screen instructions to set up the account.</p>
<ol start="3">
<li>Once account set up has been completed, or you have logged in as a returning user, you will be redirected back to the model page. A pop up window will show your new API Key. Click <em>Copy Key</em>. Be sure to paste your key somewhere safe.</li>
</ol>
<span aria-label="Expand image" class="img lightbox closed" role="button" tabindex="0"><span class="lightbox-inner"><img alt="" class="img img-align-center " height="auto" loading="lazy" src="https://files.readme.io/82c72670bdba7b1f8b9d45bf2392736d363e3b9d81215593843a70f3f732a55b-Screenshot_2025-03-18_at_18.21.08.png" title="" width="auto"/></span></span>
<ol start="4">
<li>Click the <em>Copy Code</em> button to copy the Python code given in the pane on the right. Paste that code into a Python environment or text editor.</li>
</ol>
<span aria-label="Expand image" class="img lightbox closed" role="button" tabindex="0"><span class="lightbox-inner"><img alt="" class="img  " height="auto" loading="lazy" src="https://files.readme.io/4f7c52b-image.png" title="" width="auto"/></span></span>
<ol start="5">
<li>Within the code, replace <code class="rdmd-code lang- theme-undefined" data-lang="">&quot;API_KEY_REQUIRED_IF_EXECUTING_OUTSIDE_NGC&quot;</code> with  your API key.</li>
<li>You can now run the code. Replace <code class="rdmd-code lang- theme-undefined" data-lang="">&quot;Write a limerick about the wonders of GPU computing.&quot;</code> with your own request to send to the model.</li>
</ol></div></div><div align="center" class="Flex Flex_row " justify="between" style="--flex-gap:var(--md)"><div class="UpdatedAt"><p class="DateLine "><i class="icon icon-watch"></i>Updated<!-- -->
